wake calls waiting on a replaced roots refresh\n\nTool calls waited on the refresh promise itself, so a call that started waiting on refresh A stayed blocked on A's roots/list even after a newer refresh B had installed itself and applied the client's current roots; a slow or unanswered A stalled calls until its request timed out. The barrier is now separate from the refresh: each refresh installs a new barrier before it sends roots/list and settles it when it finishes, and installing one settles the barrier it replaces, so waiting calls wake and move on to the newest. This also makes the microtask deferral from the previous commits unnecessary (the barrier is installed synchronously).
